     if patient_count is None:\n            continue\n\n        prev = best_by_code.get(code)\n        if prev is None or rev > prev[\"revision\"]:\n            best_by_code[code] = {\"revision\":      continue\n        costs[bag_size] = cost\n    return costs\n\n\ndef compute_currency(x):\n    return round(float(x), 2)\n\n\ndef main():\n    therapies = load_therapies(THERAPY_CATALOG_PATH)\n\n
